### [1] [What to Preserve, Where to Adapt: A Depth-Wise Analysis of Forgetting in Continual Gynecological Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13660)
*Amal Saqib,Tausifa Jan Saleem,Numan Saeed,Mohammad Yaqub*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究妇科医学图像分割中的持续学习遗忘问题，发现网络中更新位置会显著影响性能保持与灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床数据通常按时间顺序到达，且妇科图像在模态、解剖结构和标注规范上存在较大异质性，导致传统一次性训练假设不成立；现有持续学习方法在大分布偏移下容易发生灾难性遗忘。

Method: 作者围绕编码器—解码器分割架构开展分析：首先进行分块消融实验，评估不同网络区域对分割性能的影响；随后通过受控适应实验，限制不同深度区域可训练，观察更新位置与遗忘程度之间的关系。

Result: 早期编码器和晚期解码器被消融时性能下降最大，说明分割性能对网络层级的依赖不均衡；当训练更新限制在瓶颈附近区域时遗忘较小，而一旦浅层编码器和解码器可训练，即便更新参数量很少，遗忘也会显著增加。

Conclusion: 在编码器—解码器结构中，持续学习遗忘不仅取决于更新参数数量，更强烈受更新发生的网络深度位置影响；控制浅层编码器和解码器的更新可能是缓解妇科图像持续分割遗忘的重要方向。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation models are typically trained under the assumption that all data are available simultaneously. However, in clinical practice, datasets often arrive sequentially, requiring models to adapt continuously to evolving data distributions. We study this problem in gynecological image segmentation, where substantial heterogeneity across imaging modalities, anatomical structures, and annotation protocols creates a particularly challenging continual learning setting. Under these large distribution shifts, existing continual learning methods struggle to preserve previously learned knowledge, leading to catastrophic forgetting. To better understand forgetting in this setting, we investigate how different encoder--decoder regions influence segmentation performance and forgetting during continual gynecological segmentation. Through block-wise ablation analysis, we observe that ablating early encoder and late decoder regions results in the largest performance degradation, indicating that segmentation performance depends unevenly across the network hierarchy. Using controlled adaptation experiments, we further show that forgetting remains limited when updates are restricted to bottleneck-adjacent regions, but increases sharply once shallower encoders and decoders become trainable, even when only a small subset of parameters is updated. These findings suggest that forgetting in the encoder-decoder architecture is strongly influenced by where updates occur across network depth during continual learning. Full code and analysis pipelines will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [2] [Multiphase-Diff: Diffusion-Based Generative Modeling for High-Contrast Multiphase Physical Systems with Sharp Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13669)
*Yining Huang,Zhenyu Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Multiphase-Diff，用于高对比度、尖界面多相场的物理约束扩散生成，通过保守通量残差、解析双射潜变量表示和 Jacobi 预条件似然提升物理一致性与分布拟合质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理约束扩散方法在处理多相高对比尖界面场时面临三类耦合问题：系数跳变处强形式 PDE 残差会产生奇异梯度项并错误惩罚真实界面；极端对比下低幅值相容易被扩散噪声淹没、尺度失真或生成负系数；全局似然尺度会导致高幅值相主导监督信号。

Method: 方法包含三项设计：使用保守通量残差，避免对不连续系数求导并强化离散守恒；构造解析双射表示，将低幅值信号映射到量级为一的潜空间尺度，并通过指数解码保证系数为正；引入 Jacobi 预条件似然，对局部残差尺度归一化，实现跨相的均衡监督。

Result: 在三个互补的多相基准任务上，Multiphase-Diff 相比七个基线方法在物理保真度和分布保真度上表现更优，并在不同相对比度和相组成条件下保持较强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过将守恒型物理残差、尺度稳定的潜变量参数化和局部预条件似然结合，扩散模型可以更可靠地生成高对比、尖界面的多相科学样本。

Abstract: Physics-constrained diffusion for high-contrast, sharp-interface multiphase fields faces three coupled difficulties. At coefficient jumps, expanded pointwise strong-form PDE residuals contain singular gradient terms that can penalize physical interfaces. Under extreme contrast, low-magnitude phases may fall below the diffusion noise floor and be erased, misscaled, or generated with negative coefficients, while a global likelihood scale allows high-magnitude phases to dominate supervision. We therefore propose Multiphase-Diff, which makes three corresponding contributions: (i) a conservative flux residual that avoids differentiating discontinuous coefficients and enforces discrete conservation; (ii) an analytic bijective representation that maps low-amplitude signals to order-one latent scales and guarantees coefficient positivity through exponential decoding; and (iii) a Jacobi-preconditioned likelihood that normalizes local residual scales for balanced supervision. Experiments on three complementary multiphase benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of Multiphase-Diff over seven baselines in both physical and distributional fidelity and its robustness across phase contrasts and compositions, establishing its effectiveness for scientific sample generation in this challenging regime.

</details>


### [3] [PROVE: Training-Free Prompt Recovery using Verifiable Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13671)
*Rupayan Mallick,Mahsa Khoshnoodi,Sarah Adel Bargal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PROVE，一种无需训练、黑盒的提示词反演方法，通过组合可验证的图像场景证据来恢复提示词，在多个数据集和生成器上优于现有优化、字幕生成与强化学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本到图像模型生成能力增强，使从生成图像中反推出原始提示词变得可行，进而带来版权保护、内容归属、提示词市场中创意配方泄露与未授权复现等风险。现有方法存在可读性差、幻觉细节、对特定生成器过拟合和评估循环性等问题。

Method: 提出 PROVE（Prompt Recovery with Verified Evidence），一种训练自由、黑盒提示词反演攻击。它不直接优化 token 序列，而是从图像中提取并组合可验证的场景描述；每个恢复出的声明都需由显式图像证据支撑，并通过精度约束下的召回最大化目标进行形式化。

Result: 在 MS-COCO、Flickr30K 和 Lexica 数据集上，结合先进文本到图像生成器评估，PROVE 在图像相似度指标 DINO、LPIPS 以及文本-图像对齐指标 CLIP 上稳定超过基于优化、字幕生成和强化学习的基线方法，且无需训练、生成器访问或微调。

Conclusion: PROVE 展示了一种更强、更实用且可审计的提示词反演攻击，说明基于可验证图像证据的提示词恢复能够提高可读性、可信度和跨生成器泛化能力，同时也凸显了 AI 生成内容版权与提示词保护的现实风险。

Abstract: Modern text-to-image models can generate highly realistic images from natural-language prompts, while recent advances in prompt inversion have made it increasingly feasible to recover those prompts from generated outputs, raising new concerns for copyright protection and content ownership. As prompt marketplaces emerge, recovered prompts can enable both the unauthorized reproduction and redistribution of copyrighted creative works, and the exposure of the prompts that encode an artist's creative recipe in AI-generated content. Existing prompt inversion methods rely on gradient-based optimization, autoregressive captioning, or reinforcement learning. However, optimization-based methods often produce unreadable prompts, captioning methods hallucinate unverified details, and RL-based approaches frequently overfit to specific generators while introducing evaluation circularity. We introduce PROVE (Prompt Recovery with Verified Evidence), a training-free, black-box prompt inversion attack that reconstructs prompts by composing verifiable scene descriptions rather than optimizing token sequences, targeting both original copyrighted works and AI-generated content. The resulting prompts are fully auditable, with every recovered claim grounded in explicit image evidence, and are formalized through a precision-constrained recall maximization objective. Across MS-COCO, Flickr30K, and Lexica, using state-of-the-art text-to-image generators, PROVE consistently outperforms optimization, captioning, and RL-based baselines on image similarity (DINO, LPIPS) and text-image alignment (CLIP), without any training, generator access, or fine-tuning, demonstrating a stronger and more practical prompt inversion attack.

</details>


### [4] [MedPlex: Deep Vision-Language Co-Adaptation for Clinically Grounded Medical Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13690)
*Rafi Ibn Sultan,Hui Zhu,Chengyin Li,Dongxiao Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedPlex 是一种端到端医学视觉-语言分割框架，将文本知识持续融入编码过程，并在多类医学图像分割任务上取得领先表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像分割多被视为纯视觉问题，而临床判读依赖解剖结构、位置、外观和上下文等文本知识；已有文本引导方法通常只在后期使用语言条件，限制了语言对视觉表征学习的作用。

Method: 提出 MedPlex 框架，通过 Bi-Fusion 双向融合机制，使视觉与文本表征在编码层级中共同演化；同时引入类别级概念对齐和区域级概念对齐，分别用于锚定解剖目标的聚合临床概念画像，以及保留形状、位置、外观、纹理等细粒度概念与类别特异视觉证据的对应关系。

Result: 在 CT 和 MR 多个基准上，包括多器官、心脏子结构和肿瘤分割任务，以及真实自由文本临床监督场景中，MedPlex 达到当前最优性能。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将临床文本知识作为贯穿编码器的结构化监督，而非仅作为后期提示信号，可以显著提升医学图像分割中的视觉-语言表征质量和分割性能。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation is still largely treated as a vision-only problem, although clinical interpretation often relies on textual knowledge of anatomy, location, appearance, and surrounding context. Existing text-guided segmentation methods within the Vision-Language Model (VLM) paradigm often use language only as a late conditioning signal, limiting its influence on visual representation learning. We introduce MedPlex (Medical Plexus of Vision and Language), an end-to-end VLM framework that makes text guidance a continuous, clinically grounded component of segmentation learning. Through Bi-Fusion (Bidirectional Fusion), visual and textual representations evolve jointly across the encoding hierarchy. MedPlex further introduces class-level and region-level concept alignment to organize the shared representation at complementary granularities. Class-level alignment anchors each anatomical target to an aggregated clinical concept profile, while region-level alignment preserves individual concepts, such as shape, location, appearance, and texture, through class-specific visual evidence. In this way, language provides structured supervision throughout the encoder rather than serving only as a late-stage cue. MedPlex achieves state-of-the-art performance across CT and MR benchmarks for multi-organ, cardiac substructure, and tumor segmentation, including settings with real free-text clinical supervision. Code: https://github.com/rafiibnsultan/MedPlex.

</details>


### [5] [Limitations of Synthetic Data Generation in Specialized Data-Scarce Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13729)
*Edward Zhang,Marcel Hussing,Tanay Tandon,Shenbagaraj Kannapiran,Jason Hughes,Youkang Wang,Joshua Caswell,Agelos Kratimenos,Yi Fan Li,Milan Manoj,Ethan Sanchez,Sumukh Shrote,Camillo Jose Taylor,Daniel A. Hashimoto,Eric Eaton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散式生成模型在稀疏、高方差真实视觉领域中用于数据扩展的有效性，发现其并未稳定优于强非生成式增强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成图像生成已被用于缓解视觉任务中的数据稀缺问题，并在 ImageNet 等自然图像基准上显示潜力；但在与常见数据集差异较大、采集成本高、样本稀疏且变异性强的真实领域中，其实际收益仍不明确。

Method: 作者在五个创伤分类任务上，采用按受试者划分的训练-验证设置，比较两类生成式稀疏数据扩展方法：分布建模与样本扰动，并将其与强非生成式数据增强基线进行下游分类性能对比；同时进行特征空间分析以诊断生成数据的失效模式。

Result: 实验显示，没有任何生成式方法能够在所有任务中稳定超过强非生成式增强基线。特征空间分析发现了多种反复出现的问题，包括记忆化或模式坍缩、分布漂移，以及生成视觉上合理但过度简化、规范化的样本，这些样本比真实数据更容易被分类。

Conclusion: 在稀疏、高方差且偏离常见自然图像分布的真实视觉任务中，生成式数据扩展并不一定带来可靠收益；应谨慎评估其对下游模型的真实贡献，并关注生成样本与真实分布之间的偏差。

Abstract: Advances in diffusion-based generative models have motivated the use of synthetic image generation to alleviate data scarcity in vision tasks. While this strategy has shown promise in natural image benchmarks such as ImageNet, its effectiveness in sparse, high-variance real-world domains remains unclear. In this work, we focus on domains where images differ substantially from common image datasets and additional data are expensive to obtain. Against non-generative data augmentation baselines, we evaluate the downstream classifier performance improvements yielded by two schools of generative sparse data extension: distribution modeling and sample perturbation. Across five trauma classification tasks using subject-wise train--validation splits, no generative approach consistently outperforms a strong non-generative baseline. Feature-space analysis reveals recurring failure modes: memorization or collapse, distributional drift, and generation of visually plausible but simplified canonical instances that are easier to classify than real data.

</details>


### [6] [CAST: Closed-form Analytic Semantic Transfer for Zero-Shot Classifier Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13751)
*William Heyden,Habib Ullah,Muhammad Salman Siddiqui,Fadi Al Machot*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 CAST，一种无需训练、无需目标图像的零样本分类器扩展方法，通过闭式语义迁移将未见类语义信息注入预训练分类器权重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型预训练模型在适配新类别时通常需要目标分布样本，但许多场景中目标数据不可得；零样本学习可借助文本描述等辅助语义信息识别未见类，因此需要一种无需图像样本和迭代优化的高效适配方法。

Method: 作者提出 Closed-form Analytic Semantic Transfer（CAST）框架，以训练自由、图像自由的方式进行权重注入；同时建立理论基础，推导有限样本误差分解，并定义可计算、模型无关的 semantic extrapolation residual ρ_u，用于衡量语义外推误差。

Result: 在标准零样本学习基准上，CAST 达到或超过现有 image-free 方法，并接近少样本适配方法性能，同时不需要目标分布样本或迭代优化。

Conclusion: CAST 为预训练分类器扩展到未见类别提供了一种高效、解析化且具有理论支撑的方案；ρ_u 还可作为数据集筛选与基准设计的原则性指标。

Abstract: Large pre-trained models have become foundational components of modern machine learning systems. Yet adapting these models to novel categories typically requires examples from the target distribution. In many domains, however, such data are unavailable. Zero-shot learning (ZSL) permits recognition under these limitations through relying on auxiliary semantic information such as textual descriptions. We introduce CAST (Closed-form Analytic Semantic Transfer), a training-free, image-free framework for extending a pre-trained classifier to previously unseen classes through weight injection. We provide a theoretical foundation for CAST and derive a finite-sample error decomposition that identifies the \emph{semantic extrapolation residual} $ρ_u$. The residual is a computable, model-agnostic measure and provides a principled criterion for dataset curation and benchmark design. Experiments on standard zero-shot learning benchmarks demonstrate that CAST matches or exceeds existing image-free approaches and approaches the performance of few-shot adaptation methods, while requiring neither iterative optimization nor examples from the target distribution.

</details>


### [7] [ChartProbe: A Diagnostic Study on Visual Reasoning through Perception, Grounding, and Simple Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13766)
*Mahsa Khoshnoodi,Sarah Adel Bargal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 ChartProbe，用代码生成图表诊断探针，发现提升图表复杂视觉推理不一定需要复杂推理监督；训练感知、定位绑定与简单计算等基础技能即可显著迁移到未见复杂问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在图表问答中表现不可靠，通常被归因于复杂推理能力不足，因此多用推理监督改进。作者质疑真正瓶颈可能在推理之前的基础视觉技能：读取图表元素、将元素与标签正确绑定，以及执行排名、求和、求差等单步运算。

Method: 提出 ChartProbe 诊断框架：直接从渲染图表的代码生成探针与精确答案，无需人工标注，并可将错误归因到单一技能。实验中不使用复杂问题和推理链训练，而是分别对单一基础技能进行微调，再评估其对保留的复杂推理问题的迁移效果。

Result: 在三个开源 VLM 上，仅用基础技能监督即可在未训练过的复杂推理问题上获得显著提升。效果还可泛化到三类分布外设置：未见过的饼图类型、与图像和模板不重合的 ChartQA 人写基准，以及非图表视觉域 CLEVR。

Conclusion: 复杂视觉推理能力的提升并不必然依赖复杂推理监督；当模型的低层视觉读取、定位绑定和简单计算能力较弱且可被训练时，强化这些基础技能可以以较低监督成本恢复大量复杂推理表现。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) remain unreliable on chart questions that require reasoning over visual quantities, and this weakness is usually attributed to a reasoning deficit and addressed with more reasoning supervision. We ask whether the difficulty lies in reasoning itself, or in the simpler skills that reasoning operates on: reading the plotted elements (\emph{perception}), locating them and binding them to their labels (\emph{grounding}), and performing single-step computations such as ranking, totals, and differences (\emph{simple reasoning}). We introduce \textbf{ChartProbe}, a diagnostic framework whose probes are generated directly from the code that renders each chart, so every gold answer is exact by construction, needs no human annotation, and attributes each failure to a single skill. ChartProbe enables an intervention prior work does not attempt: instead of synthesizing complex-reasoning data, we withhold complex questions and reasoning traces entirely, fine-tune on one simple skill at a time, and measure transfer to held-out complex-reasoning questions. Across three open-weight VLMs, supervising the simpler skills alone produces large gains on complex-reasoning questions the model never trained on: where these skills are weak and the model can be taught to read the image, training them recovers much of complex reasoning at no reasoning-data cost. The gains hold across three out-of-distribution settings: an unseen chart type (pie charts), a human-written benchmark disjoint from our images and templates (ChartQA), and a non-chart visual domain (CLEVR). Complex visual reasoning can therefore improve without complex-reasoning supervision.

</details>


### [8] [Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks for Spatially Independent Multispectral Land Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13769)
*Katherine L. Bauer,Teemu Harkonen,Simo Sarkka,Arturo Sanchez-Azofeifa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估Kolmogorov-Arnold Network（KAN）在Landsat 8多光谱卫星影像土地分类中的表现，并与随机森林和多层感知机比较；KAN在独立Calgary数据集上达到与随机森林相当的准确率、优于MLP，同时参数更少、可解释性更强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土地分类对土地管理、环境监测和城市规划具有重要意义。已有随机森林和多层感知机在多光谱数据上表现较好，而KAN作为一种结构紧凑、具有潜在可解释性的替代神经网络架构，尚需验证其在遥感土地分类任务中的有效性。

Method: 使用Landsat 8影像数据，基于Edmonton数据训练和测试模型，并在独立Calgary数据集上进行泛化评估。比较模型包括KAN、随机森林和多层感知机，分类类别为农业、城市、水体、森林和裸地五类。

Result: 在Calgary独立测试数据上，KAN的分类准确率与随机森林相当，并优于多层感知机；同时KAN所需可训练参数显著更少，并提供更好的可解释性。

Conclusion: KAN是土地覆盖/土地利用分类任务中一种有竞争力的模型选择，尤其适用于需要较小模型规模和较强可解释性的遥感应用场景。

Abstract: Land classification from satellite imagery is important for land management, environmental monitoring, and urban planning. Machine learning methods such as random forests and multilayer perceptrons have shown strong performance on multispectral data, while the Kolmogorov-Arnold network has emerged as an alternative architecture with compact model structures. This study evaluates the Kolmogorov-Arnold network for land classification using Landsat 8 imagery and compares it with random forest and multilayer perceptron models. The models were trained and tested on data from Edmonton, Alberta and evaluated on an independent dataset from Calgary, Alberta across five land classes: agriculture, urban, water, forest, and bare ground. For the Calgary dataset, the Kolmogorov-Arnold network matched the accuracy of the random forest and outperformed the multilayer perceptron, while requiring substantially fewer trainable parameters and providing greater interpretability.

</details>


### [9] [Doomed to Re-Annotate, Forever: The ImageNet Story](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13783)
*Illia Volkov,Nikita Kisel,Tetiana Mishkina,Klara Janouskova,Jiri Matas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 ReImageNet，对 ImageNet-1k 验证集进行大规模、多轮高质量重标注，发现原始标签存在显著错误，并证明更准确的多标签与语义标注会改变模型评估结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ImageNet-1k 的 Top-1 准确率仍是视觉识别领域最常用指标，但其原始 2012 标签长期存在噪声、类别定义不清、多对象图像处理不足等问题；已有修正工作不够全面，因此需要更准确、完整、可复现的验证集标注。

Method: 作者构建 ReImageNet 标注体系，包含多标签修正、目标定位、类别定义修订和语义属性标注，并采用人类与 LLM 协作、多轮 refinement、错误检查和工具辅助流程，以应对 ImageNet 规模下的一次性标注不可行问题。

Result: 重标注发现约 12% 原始标签错误，33.3% 图像为多标签，3.8% 图像不包含任何 ImageNet-1k 类别目标。使用新标签后，监督模型 Top-1 准确率最高提升 1.2%，多模态大语言模型提升约 5–6%。同时发现 ImageNet-1k 的问题会传播到其衍生测试集。

Conclusion: ImageNet-1k 的标注问题具有结构性，影响主流模型评估的可靠性。ReImageNet 通过多轮人机协作标注提供了更高质量的验证基准，并公开全部标注、类别定义、指南和分析代码，有助于提升视觉识别评测的准确性与透明度。

Abstract: Top-1 accuracy on ImageNet-1k remains the most commonly reported metric in visual recognition. Quality issues with the dataset have been repeatedly reported, yet the original 2012 noisy labels are still predominantly used. The paper presents a comprehensive effort, which goes well beyond prior correction attempts, towards obtaining accurate and complete ImageNet-1k validation set annotations. The result, ReImageNet, includes multilabel correction, object localization, revised class definitions, and semantic attributes (text-recognition, rendition, reflection, crowd, dominant). The reannotation reveals that approximately 12% of the original ImageNet-1k labels are incorrect, 33.3% of images are multilabel and 3.8% contain no object from an ImageNet-1k class. With the new labels, top-1 accuracy increases by up to 1.2% for supervised models and by 5-6% for MLLMs. We argue that annotation at ImageNet scale cannot realistically be completed in one pass, as errors and definitional issues are discovered only through annotating, and we build our pipeline around repeated refinement and error checking. We observed that human and LLM collaboration with appropriate tooling represents the current quality ceiling for annotation at this scale. ImageNet-1k issues propagate into its derivative test sets, indicating that the problem is structural rather than specific to any single benchmark. All annotations, class definitions, guidelines, and analysis code have been publicly released. Project page: https://vrg.fel.cvut.cz/reimagenet Annotations: https://huggingface.co/datasets/vrg-prague/ReImageNet Code: https://github.com/klarajanouskova/ImageNet

</details>


### [10] [Face Re-morphing: Differential Morphing Attack Detection via Feature-Space Similarity Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13858)
*Jie Jin,Masakatsu Nishigaki,Tetsushi Ohki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Face Re-morphing：通过对证件照与可信现场照执行额外重变形，并观察相似度变化来检测人脸变形攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脸变形攻击可使同一张证件图像匹配多个贡献者，威胁人脸识别系统；现有 D-MAD 方法多依赖静态特征差异、成分脸重建或多线索融合，仍需更具互补性的检测线索。

Method: 给定证件图像和可信现场图像，生成 re-morphed 图像；计算证件图像-现场图像与现场图像-re-morphed 图像之间的余弦相似度变化，并将该变化作为检测分数。

Result: 在 FRLL-Morphs 和 FEI Morph 上，该线索在不同变形条件、重变形方法和人脸识别模型下均有效；与已有方法相比，在 AMSL 上表现较好，并在 FEI Morph Version 1 的 Criminal 条件下，尤其结合 MorDIFF 时表现突出。

Conclusion: 重变形诱导的相似度变化可作为 D-MAD 的有效且互补的检测线索，有助于提升对人脸变形攻击的识别能力。

Abstract: Face morphing attacks pose a serious threat to face recognition systems because a single morphed document image can be matched to multiple contributors. Differential morphing attack detection (D-MAD) addresses this threat by comparing a document image with a trusted live image, but existing methods often rely on static feature differences, constituent-face reconstruction, or multi-cue fusion. This paper proposes Face Re-morphing, a D-MAD method that uses the feature-space response to an additional morphing operation as a detection cue. Given a document image and a trusted live image, the proposed method generates a re-morphed image and uses the change between the document--live and live--re-morphed cosine similarities as the detection score. Experiments on FRLL-Morphs and FEI Morph show that the proposed cue is effective across different morphing conditions, re-morphing methods, and face recognition models. Comparisons with existing methods show favorable results on AMSL and indicate that the proposed method performs well under the Criminal condition on FEI Morph Version~1, particularly when using MorDIFF. These results indicate that re-morphing-induced similarity change provides a complementary cue for D-MAD.

</details>


### [11] [XSA-MAD: Cross-modal Semantic Alignment for Morphing Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13861)
*Jie Jin,Mahiro Tokumasu,Yu Makino,Masakatsu Nishigaki,Tetsushi Ohki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于 CLIP 的多模态人脸变形攻击检测框架 XSA-MAD，通过文本语义属性建模提升对未知变形生成技术的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于图像的人脸变形攻击检测方法主要依赖视觉线索，面对未见过的生成技术时泛化较差；因此需要一种能捕获生成方式无关、概念层面差异的检测方法。

Method: 将变形相关概念分解为身份、面部几何、纹理和一致性四类可解释属性，并构造结构化、属性感知的文本表示；利用 CLIP 框架将图像编码器逐步对齐到判别性文本语义空间，形成统一语义表征。

Result: 在 SMDD 上训练，并在 MAD22 和 MorDIFF 上测试，XSA-MAD 在多种变形原则下表现出较强泛化能力；在 GAN 变形样本上达到 2.92% 的等错误率，并在高保真生成攻击场景中持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: XSA-MAD 通过显式建模真实与变形人脸之间的语义不一致，有效提升了跨生成技术的人脸变形攻击检测性能，说明多模态语义对齐有助于构建更稳健的 MAD 方法。

Abstract: Morphing attacks pose a serious threat to face recognition systems. However, existing image-based morphing attack detection (MAD) methods often generalize poorly to unseen generation techniques because they rely solely on visual cues. We propose XSA-MAD, a CLIP-based multimodal framework that explicitly models semantic inconsistencies between bona-fide and morphed faces. Morphing concepts are decomposed into four interpretable attributes, including identity, facial geometry, texture, and consistency, and are encoded as structured and attribute-aware textual representations. The image encoder is progressively aligned with this discriminative textual space, resulting in a unified semantic representation that captures generation-invariant and concept-level discrepancies between bona-fide and morph images. Experiments on MAD22 and MorDIFF, following training on SMDD, demonstrate strong generalization across diverse morphing principles. In particular, XSA-MAD achieves an equal error rate of 2.92% on GAN-based morphs and consistently outperforms existing methods under high-fidelity generative attacks.

</details>


### [12] [Attention Capture Is Not Detection: A Two-Stage Account of How Humans Miss Localized AI Image Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13865)
*Chiao-Chieh Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文指出 AI 图像编辑的可检测性并非单一属性，而是由“是否被注意到”和“是否被正确判断为伪造”两个可分离阶段构成；眼动实验与生成式扫视路径模型共同支持这一两阶段框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有平台通常将 AI 图像编辑检测视为二元问题：要么标注警告，要么不标注。作者认为这种做法忽略了人类感知与判断过程中的阶段差异，尤其是注意捕获与语义判断可能受不同因素影响，因此需要更细粒度地理解 AI 编辑图像的可检测性。

Method: 研究包含一个受控眼动实验，样本量为 N=59，采用拉丁方设计，设置四种条件，交叉操纵编辑区域大小与语义合理性。作者使用混合效应模型分析编辑是否被注意到、是否被正确判定为伪造以及 LBFS（look-but-fail-to-see，即看到了但未识别）错误率。同时，训练一个基于 Transformer 的生成式眼动模型来生成扫视路径，并评估其对图像级注意分布和 LBFS 发生率的预测能力，与二参数线性基线进行比较。

Result: 实验显示，编辑区域大小显著影响注意捕获（p<0.001），而语义合理性显著影响判断准确率和 LBFS 错误率（p<0.001）。多重比较校正后，该两阶段分离结果仍然成立；区域大小与语义合理性的次级交互效应不显著。Transformer 模型在预测每张图像的注意模式上表现较强，Pearson r=0.77–0.82；在预测 LBFS 发生率上，即使不使用语义合理性标签，也略优于线性基线（r=0.52 vs. r=0.48）。

Conclusion: 论文提出 AI 编辑图像可检测性的两阶段解释：低层或显著性相关因素更影响注意捕获，而语义合理性更影响伪造判断与漏识别。该框架将视觉注意研究中“前注意捕获”和“努力性识别”的区分扩展到 AI 图像编辑检测领域。模型结果表明生成式眼动模型可部分操作化注意捕获阶段，但作者也承认验证设置有限，如仅使用固定训练/验证划分而非留一被试交叉验证，因此结论较为审慎。

Abstract: As AI-generated image edits proliferate, the platforms meant to curb the resulting disinformation treat detectability as a single, undifferentiated property: an edit either gets a warning or it does not. We show this is the wrong model. Across a controlled eye-tracking study ($N=59$, Latin-square design, four conditions crossing edit area and semantic plausibility), a mixed-effects analysis reveals that whether an edit is noticed and whether it is correctly judged as fake are dissociable stages, governed by different factors: edit area drives attention capture ($p<0.001$) while semantic plausibility drives judgment accuracy and look-but-fail-to-see (LBFS) error rates ($p<0.001$). This dissociation survives correction for multiple comparisons; a secondary interaction between the two factors does not. This two-stage account extends a long-standing distinction in visual attention research (between pre-attentive capture and effortful recognition) into the new domain of AI-edit detectability. We then test whether a generative eye-movement model can computationally operationalize the attention-capture stage: a Transformer trained to generate scanpaths tracks per-image attention with strong discriminative power (Pearson $r=0.77$--$0.82$ across held-out stimuli) and, on the harder task of predicting LBFS incidence, modestly outperforms a two-parameter linear baseline even without access to the plausibility label ($r=0.52$ vs. $r=0.48$). We report this comparison, our ablations, and our method's limitations (a single fixed train/validation split, not leave-one-subject-out) without inflation, consistent with responsibly communicating what a machine learning system can and cannot do to help curb AI-driven disinformation.

</details>


### [13] [Consensus-gated Multi-Agent Neural Architecture Search for Seismic Fault Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13889)
*Shehram Baig,Ahmad Mustafa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种由多个大语言模型协同评审与生成代码的代理式神经架构搜索系统，在极少候选训练和有限算力下发现小型高效的地震断层分割网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地震断层分割标注数据稀缺，直接借用计算机视觉或医学影像网络并不一定数据最优；人工设计架构耗时，传统NAS依赖人工搜索空间且计算成本高，因此需要低成本、适合地球物理小数据场景的自动架构发现方法。

Method: 构建由Claude、GPT-5.1和Gemini 2.5 Pro组成的LLM评审小组，对候选架构进行辩论直至一致；系统自动撰写完整PyTorch代码、交叉审查，并接入验证-训练-评分闭环。约束包括45万参数上限、保留或回退谱系机制以及失败机制记忆。搜索直接操作源代码而非固定操作菜单，在单张消费级GPU上训练8个候选。

Result: 系统发现一个42.5万参数的编码器-解码器模型，包含条带池化瓶颈、SE门控、非对称单卷积解码器和特征金字塔融合颈部。在Thebe断层数据集派生切片上，以相同训练协议比较，该模型取得最高F1=0.578和最高IoU，同时参数量最小，优于31M参数U-Net、39.6M参数DeepLabV3-ResNet50和1.83M参数Attention U-Net等基线。搜索成本为101次LLM调用、约115万输入token和39万输出token，以及约1个GPU日。

Conclusion: 共识门控的LLM多智能体NAS可在小数据、低算力、严格参数预算下实现面向领域的高效架构发现，为地震断层分割等标注稀缺任务提供了一条实用且低成本的模型设计路线。

Abstract: Neural networks for seismic fault segmentation are often borrowed from computer vision and medical imaging domains where they train under relatively much larger labeled data resources. Optimizing their architecture under tight labeled data budgets as are common in geophysical applications is not a trivial problem. Manually designing data-optimal architectures is time-consuming while classical neural architecture search (NAS) is restricted to hand-crafted search spaces and large compute budgets. We present an agentic NAS system in which a panel of three large language models (Claude, GPT-5.1, and Gemini~2.5~Pro) debates each candidate architecture to unanimous consensus, authors the complete PyTorch implementation, cross-reviews it, and submits it to an automated validate-train-score loop with a hard 450K parameter budget, keep-or-revert lineage, and a memory of failed mechanisms. Operating on source code rather than a predefined operation menu, the search ran on a single consumer GPU and trained only eight candidates. It discovered \ours{}: a 425K-parameter encoder-decoder with a strip-pooling bottleneck, squeeze-and-excitation gating, an asymmetric one-conv decoder, and a feature-pyramid fusion neck. Trained under a protocol identical to all baselines on sections derived from the Thebe fault dataset, it attains the highest F1 (0.578) and IoU of all models tested while being the smallest, outperforming a published-capacity U-Net (31M parameters, F1 0.484), DeepLabV3-ResNet50 (39.6M, 0.516), an Attention U-Net(1.83M, 0.502). The search cost 101 LLM calls ($\sim$1.15M input / 0.39M output tokens) and roughly one GPU-day, making consensus-gated LLM panels a practical, low-cost route to domain-specific architecture discovery.

</details>


### [14] [Beyond Control Points: Arcsecond Relative-Motion Estimation of Vision Measurement Platforms With Incomplete or Absent Control Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13918)
*Meng Lian,Jian Wang,Shuixin Pan,Haibo Liu,Yueqiang Zhang,Yulan Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种控制自适应的视觉差分监测框架，可直接由图像位移和已知3D点估计相邻帧间相机平台运动，在无专用控制点或少量控制点条件下实现高精度、快速、鲁棒的变形监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远距离视觉变形监测对相机平台运动极其敏感；传统绝对位姿差分方法依赖专用控制数据，并会把两次独立位姿估计误差传播到相对运动估计中，影响精度与鲁棒性。

Method: 作者构建控制自适应差分框架：无专用控制点时由测量点观测恢复平台旋转；一个测量控制点时通过先验约束恢复平移；两个非平行控制射线时恢复完整三维平移。方法不需要非线性优化或初始位姿估计，并推导旋转可观测条件、未建模平移/非刚性点运动泄漏界以及单点轴向先验偏差规律。

Result: 在0.5像素图像噪声、最大30角分姿态变化和最大2 mm三维点扰动下，多相机估计器旋转RMSE为2.97角秒，平均运行时间0.46 ms；单控制点下先验约束平移RMSE为1.19 mm；桥梁实验中相对于全站仪的坐标分量位移RMSE中位数为0.85 mm；在公共RGB-D和双目序列的3D坐标扰动测试中保持零发散。

Conclusion: 该框架在控制数据稀缺或不稳定场景下仍能实现高精度平台运动补偿与结构位移测量，兼具标定鲁棒性和计算效率，在所评估方法中达到先进水平。

Abstract: Long-range vision-based deformation monitoring is highly sensitive to motion of the camera platform. Absolute-pose differencing typically relies on dedicated control data and propagates two independent pose errors into the relative-motion estimate. We develop a control-adaptive differential framework that estimates inter-frame platform motion directly from image displacements and known 3D points. With no dedicated control point, the framework recovers platform rotation from measurement-point observations. One surveyed control point enables prior-constrained translation recovery, while two nonparallel control rays recover full 3D translation. The framework requires neither nonlinear optimization nor an initial pose estimate. Excluding control data from the rotation stage makes the rotation estimate exactly immune to contamination confined to the control field. The inherited differential formulation also cancels translational extrinsic errors exactly. We derive the rotation observability condition, a leakage bound for unmodeled translation and nonrigid point motion, and the single-point axial-prior bias law. Under 0.5-pixel image noise, attitude changes of up to 30~arcmin, and 3D point perturbations of up to 2~mm, the multi-camera estimator achieves a rotation RMSE of 2.97~arcsec and an average runtime of 0.46~ms. With one surveyed control point, its prior-constrained translation RMSE is 1.19~mm. In a bridge experiment without a stable control field, the median coordinate-wise displacement RMSE relative to total-station measurements is 0.85~mm. The estimator also maintains zero divergence under the tested 3D coordinate perturbations on public RGB-D and stereo sequences. These results establish state-of-the-art accuracy, calibration robustness, and computational efficiency among the evaluated methods.

</details>


### [15] [OpenBelief-Nav: Evidence-Preserving Object Memory for Open-Vocabulary Language-Guided Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13923)
*Dinh Tuan Nguyen,Anh Dao,Phuong Nam Dang,Quan-Dung Pham,Tuyen P. Le,Truong Nguyen,Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OpenBelief-Nav通过保留观测级语义证据与对象信念，减少早期语义承诺造成的信息丢失，并在3D场景图投影与导航任务中取得改进或相当表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇3D场景图通常将对象表示为单一融合特征或固定语义标签，过早承诺可能丢弃少数但与任务相关的语义假设，影响语言引导导航中的目标检索与决策。

Method: 提出 evidence-preserving object memory：保留观测级短语、可靠性线索和帧-掩码来源，同时分离聚合几何表示与视觉表示；将语义相关短语整合为与词表无关的对象信念，并支持任务时的固定词表投影或自由形式检索。

Result: 在5个ScanNet200和8个Replica场景上，full-belief projection 的mIoU分别为0.2742和0.2912，高于匹配的early-commit readout的0.2393和0.2701；在78次HM3D-YCB导航试验中，consensus与early-commit retrieval均为60/78成功，belief-weighted为58/78，DualMap为55/78；在20次Unitree G1运行、10个匹配评测案例中，允许最多两次验证候选尝试的纠错策略将目标确认成功率从6/10提升到8/10。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，在开放词汇对象记忆中保留多源证据与不确定语义假设，有助于提升语义投影质量，并可通过候选验证与纠错机制改善真实机器人目标确认表现。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary 3D scene graphs provide compact semantic memory for language-guided navigation, but mapped objects are often exposed through a single fused feature or committed semantic label. Such commitment can remove minority yet task-relevant hypotheses from the task-time interface. We present OpenBelief-Nav, an evidence-preserving object memory that retains observation-level phrases, reliability cues, and frame-mask provenance while maintaining separate aggregate geometric and visual representations. Semantically related phrases are consolidated into a vocabulary-independent object belief from which task-specific readouts perform fixed-vocabulary projection or free-form retrieval. On five ScanNet200 and eight Replica scenes, full-belief projection achieves mIoU scores of 0.2742 and 0.2912, compared with 0.2393 and 0.2701 for a matched early-commit readout. Across 78 HM3D-YCB navigation trials, consensus and early-commit retrieval each achieve 60/78 successes, compared with 58/78 for belief-weighted retrieval and 55/78 for DualMap. Across 20 Unitree G1 runs organized as 10 matched evaluation cases, a correction policy permitting at most two verified candidate attempts improves target-confirmation success from 6/10 to 8/10 relative to top-1-only execution. Code will be released upon acceptance at https://openbelief-nav.github.io/.

</details>


### [16] [RGBX-Next: Towards Realistic Generative Rendering from G-Buffers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13929)
*Zheng Zeng,Marco Salvi,Lifan Wu,Jan Novák,Daqi Lin,Saeed Hadadan,Yichen Sheng,Robert Pottorff,Shiqiu Liu,Ravi Ramamoorthi,Ling-Qi Yan,Miloš Hašan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RGBX-Next 将扩散 Transformer 微调为统一的生成式正向/逆向渲染器，可在图像、视频与流式数据中实现 G-buffer 估计与逼真渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在图像、视频和流式生成中效果突出，但相比传统 3D 渲染缺乏对输出的精确控制。作者希望通过以传统渲染得到的 G-buffer 作为条件，将生成模型转化为可控的学习型渲染器。

Method: 提出 RGBX-Next 统一框架，用于正向渲染与逆向渲染；核心方法是给出一种通用微调流程，将 DiT 模型适配为生成式正向渲染器和逆向渲染器，使其既能从 G-buffer 生成真实图像/视频/流，也能从图像/视频/流估计 G-buffer。

Result: 摘要称该方法在真实感生成渲染和 intrinsic decomposition（内在属性分解/G-buffer 估计）两方面均达到较高质量，并计划公开所有模型。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了将扩散模型与传统渲染表征结合的可行性，为可控的生成式正向与逆向渲染提供了统一设计范式。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved impressive results in image, video, and streaming generation. However, compared to traditional 3D rendering, they still lack precise control over the generated output. We believe a viable path forward is to use generative models as learned renderers conditioned on traditionally rendered G-buffers. We introduce RGBX-Next, a unified generative framework for forward and inverse rendering, which allows estimating G-buffers from images, videos, and streams, and rendering realistic images, videos, and streams from G-buffers. Our key contribution is a general recipe for finetuning diffusion transformer (DiT) models into generative forward and inverse renderers. We show that the resulting models achieve high quality in both realistic generative rendering and intrinsic decomposition. We will make all our models publicly available. We believe that the design principles presented in this paper will benefit future research on controllable generative forward and inverse rendering.

</details>


### [17] [CoANeRV: Coordinate-Aware Token-Space Neural Video Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13938)
*Jialong Guo,Ke Liu,Mengxuan Li,Jiajun Bu,Haishuai Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoANeRV是一种用于摊销式视频神经表示的坐标感知 token 空间框架，通过一次前向传播生成紧凑视频 token，并用共享坐标条件解码器进行连续时空重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 NeRV/INR 类视频表示通常需要昂贵的逐视频优化，或为每个视频生成专用网络权重，难以扩展到高效的摊销式视频表示；同时，高分辨率坐标解码还面临显存开销问题。

Method: 提出 CoANeRV：首先以前馈方式形成视频 token；随后使用共享的坐标条件解码器，根据连续时空坐标查询重建视频。其核心设计包括轴自适应位置编码、温度调制交叉注意力以对齐时空查询与视频 token，以及分块坐标查询来降低峰值注意力显存。

Result: 在多个视频数据集上，CoANeRV 相比既有前馈 NeRV 和 INR 基线获得更好的重建质量；相比注意力式坐标解码器降低峰值显存；并实现无需逐视频优化的高效摊销编码。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将前馈 token 形成、时空坐标检索与显存受限的密集查询结合起来，可以有效提升视频神经表示在重建质量、编码效率和高分辨率实用性之间的平衡。

Abstract: Neural representations for videos (NeRV) have shown strong reconstruction fidelity by storing video-specific information in network weights. However, existing formulations typically require either costly per-video optimization or video-specific weight generation, making it difficult to scale to efficient amortized video representation. We propose CoANeRV, a coordinate-aware token-space framework that adapts the broader token-conditioned neural-field paradigm to amortized video representation. CoANeRV forms compact video tokens in one feed-forward pass and uses a shared coordinate-conditioned decoder to reconstruct continuous spatio-temporal queries, avoiding per-video decoder optimization or generation while retaining coordinate-level reconstruction flexibility. To make token-space reconstruction effective, CoANeRV introduces a coordinate-aware decoding architecture that aligns spatio-temporal queries with video tokens through axis-adaptive positional encoding and temperature-modulated cross-attention. Block-wise coordinate querying further reduces peak attention memory, making high-resolution reconstruction practical. Experiments on diverse video datasets show that CoANeRV consistently improves reconstruction quality over prior feed-forward NeRV and INR baselines, reduces peak memory compared with attention-based coordinate decoders, and provides efficient amortized encoding without per-video optimization. These results support the proposed video-specific combination of feed-forward token formation, spatio-temporal coordinate retrieval, and memory-bounded dense querying. The code is available at https://github.com/jialong2023/CoANeRV.

</details>


### [18] [CMCNet: Aligning Ultrasound Image Embeddings with Textual TI-RADS Representations for Fine-Grained Thyroid Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13939)
*Bingxin Yu,Xueli Wang,Jerry Zhou,Wenyan Wang,Li Wen,Lan Huang,Xin Feng,Fengfeng Zhou,Kewei Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出含600个甲状腺结节的STN数据集，并提出CMCNet，通过将超声图像嵌入对齐到由TI-RADS标准化特征描述生成的固定文本嵌入，实现更高效、鲁棒的多类别风险分级预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有甲状腺结节超声深度学习研究多集中于良恶性二分类，较少处理ACR TI-RADS的多等级风险预测；同时，TI-RADS五类特征的显式监督利用不足，主要受限于完整特征标注数据缺乏。

Method: 构建STN数据集，包含600个甲状腺结节的横断面与纵断面超声图像、边界框标注以及五类TI-RADS特征标签。方法上模拟临床决策流程，在训练阶段利用结构化特征信息；将标准化TI-RADS特征描述转换为文本嵌入，作为风险等级的稳定替代表示；提出CMCNet，使用Center-Margin Contrastive Loss将图像嵌入对齐到固定文本嵌入，同时增强类内紧凑性与类间分离性，推理阶段仅需图像输入。

Result: 实验显示，该嵌入对齐策略相比直接多任务学习更具数据效率和鲁棒性，并持续优于InfoNCE、center loss、强多任务基线以及VQA式多模态模型，尤其在类别不均衡场景下表现更好。

Conclusion: 利用TI-RADS结构化特征描述形成文本语义中心，并通过对比式中心边界约束指导图像表示学习，是一种适合小样本和类别不均衡医学影像多分类任务的有效方案；STN数据集和代码公开有助于推动该方向研究。

Abstract: Ultrasound is the primary imaging modality for assessing thyroid nodules, and the ACR TI-RADS framework standardizes diagnosis through five ultrasound feature categories that are aggregated into five risk levels (TR1-TR5). Although widely adopted in clinical practice, most deep learning approaches focus on binary malignancy classification, while multi-class prediction and explicit utilization of feature-level supervision remain underexplored, largely due to limited annotated data. In this study, we introduce the STN dataset of 600 thyroid nodules with paired transverse and longitudinal ultrasound images, bounding box annotations, and complete labels for all five TI-RADS feature categories. Following the clinical decision process, we investigate how structured feature information can guide representation learning during training while requiring only images at inference. We demonstrate that text embeddings derived from standardized feature descriptions form a stable surrogate representation for TI-RADS risk levels. Based on this observation, we propose CMCNet, which aligns image embeddings to fixed textual embeddings via a Center-Margin Contrastive Loss that simultaneously promotes intra-class compactness and inter-class separation. Experimental results show that this embedding alignment strategy is more data-efficient and robust than direct multitask learning, and consistently outperforms InfoNCE, center loss, a strong multitask baseline, and a VQA-style multimodal model, particularly in imbalanced settings. The dataset is freely available at doi: 10.5281/zenodo.19125693 and the source code is available at: https://www.healthinformaticslab.org/supp/.

</details>


### [19] [Fast Implicit Neural Light Field Representation via Geometric Decomposition and Multi-Resolution Low-Rank Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13949)
*Yao Guo,Ligen Shi,Shuchen Sun,Jun Qiu,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种基于几何分解与多分辨率低秩特征的快速隐式光场表示，用更少参数和更短训练/推理时间实现有竞争力的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式神经表示可紧凑、连续地重建密集光场，但光场是高维信号，具有强空间-角度冗余和结构化视差变化。直接用神经网络拟合4D光线坐标通常优化耗时，且难以同时恢复视图外观与跨视图一致性。

Method: 将4D光场分解为水平视差平面、空间纹理平面和垂直视差平面。每个平面采用低秩结构表示：在多个分辨率层级上，将低分辨率2D网格与两个高分辨率1D线特征的逐元素乘积结合；融合后的特征输入轻量级MLP解码以预测RGB值。

Result: 在公开光场数据集上的实验表明，该方法在重建质量上具有竞争力，同时在模型参数量、训练时间和推理效率之间取得更优折中。

Conclusion: 几何分解结合多分辨率低秩特征能够有效利用光场的空间-角度冗余和视差结构，从而提升隐式光场重建的效率与实用性。

Abstract: Implicit neural representations provide a compact and continuous way to reconstruct dense light fields from sampled ray coordinates. However, fast light field reconstruction remains challenging because a light field is a high-dimensional signal with strong spatial-angular redundancy and structured disparity variations. Directly fitting 4D ray coordinates with a neural network often requires considerable optimization time to recover both view appearance and cross-view consistency. To address this issue, this paper proposes a fast implicit light field representation based on geometric decomposition and multi-resolution low-rank features. The proposed method decomposes a 4D light field into a horizontal disparity plane, a spatial texture plane, and a vertical disparity plane. Each plane is represented by a low-rank structure that combines a low-resolution 2D grid with the element-wise product of two high-resolution 1D line features at multiple resolution levels. The fused features are decoded by a lightweight multilayer perceptron to predict RGB values. Experiments on public light field datasets show that the proposed method achieves competitive reconstruction quality while providing a better trade-off among model parameters, training time, and inference efficiency.

</details>


### [20] [SAFE: Scene-Aware Feature Modulation for Color Constancy with Learned Color Space in Pure-Color Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13967)
*Yuan-Kang Lee,Kuan-Lin Chen,Chih-Heng Chang,Jian-Jiun Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对纯色场景中的颜色恒常性估计难题，提出结合 SAFE 特征调制网络与 LCS 学习颜色空间的紧凑框架，以缓解色度线索坍缩并提升光照估计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在纯色场景中，大多数像素集中在狭窄色相范围内，基于色度的传统线索会坍缩为单一点，导致标准颜色恒常性估计器存在严重歧义。因此需要一种能根据场景复杂度自适应利用光照线索、并改善纯色场景色度表达的方法。

Method: 方法包含两部分：第一，SAFE（Scene-Aware FeaturE modulation）将光照线索组织为结构化的四 token 表征，并依据场景复杂度特征对这些 token 进行选择性重加权；第二，LCS（Learned Color Space）进行场景相关的色度归一化，专门缓解纯色场景中的色度坍缩问题。

Result: 实验显示 SAFE 在纯色场景中稳定提升性能。相较各指标下最佳基线，平均角误差降低 10%，最佳 25% 样本误差降低 20%，最差 25% 样本误差降低 5.8%。

Conclusion: 该框架通过场景感知的特征调制与学习型颜色空间归一化，有效增强了纯色场景下颜色恒常性估计的鲁棒性与准确性，说明针对场景复杂度和色度坍缩问题的显式建模是有效的。

Abstract: Color constancy on pure-color scenes is challenging: when most pixels share a narrow band of hues, every chromaticity-based cue collapses to a single point and standard estimators become ambiguous. We propose a compact framework that couples two innovations: (i) SAFE, a Scene-Aware FeaturE modulation network that organizes illumination cues into a structured four-token representation, which is then selectively reweighted based on scene complexity features; (ii) the Learned Color Space (LCS), a scene-dependent chromaticity normalization that directly addresses the chromaticity collapse problem for pure-color scenes. Experiment results show that SAFE consistently improves performance in pure-color scenes. Compared to the best-performing baseline in each metric, it reduces the mean angular error by 10%, the best-25% error by 20%, and the worst-25% error by 5.8%.

</details>


### [21] [PPOM: Marginalizing Patch-Grid Phase for CLIP-Based Generalizable Vision-Language Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13969)
*Liang Wang,Haoyang Li,Chao Wang,Guodong Long,Jing Jiang,Yan Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PPOM，一种无需训练的推理算子，通过对图像相对于ViT patch网格的相位偏移进行边缘化，降低CLIP类视觉语言模型在提示调优后对patch对齐的敏感性，并在多个prompt-learning方法上提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP式视觉语言模型经提示调优后仅需少量可训练参数，但其视觉编码器通常冻结；ViT的非重叠patch切分会导致预测依赖图像与patch网格之间的对齐相位，从而产生空间采样敏感性。论文旨在缓解这种由patch-grid alignment引起的预测不稳定问题。

Method: 提出Patch-Phase Orbit Marginalization（PPOM）：在推理阶段将patch相位偏移视为 nuisance variable。给定patch stride，对原始视图和经反射填充后的平移视图进行评估；将相反方向的平移配对为水平、垂直和对角的antithetic families；对这些相位族与原始预测赋予相等权重，以避免由于视图数量不同造成的偏置，从而完成相位积分/边缘化。

Result: 在多个prompt-learning宿主方法上，PPOM无需重新训练即可提升宿主模型性能，说明其作为训练后推理增强策略具有一定通用性。

Conclusion: PPOM为提示适配模型与patch网格敏感性之间提供了确定性的推理接口，可有效降低由ViT非重叠patch采样带来的相位对齐依赖，并作为即插即用方法改善CLIP类模型表现。

Abstract: Prompt tuning adapts CLIP-based vision-language models with few trainable parameters, yet its predictions remain sensitive to the spatial sampling imposed by a frozen vision transformer. In particular, non-overlapping patch tokenization makes predictions depend on the alignment (phase) between image and the patch lattice. To reduce prediction sensitivity to patch-grid alignment, we introduce Patch-Phase Orbit Marginalization (PPOM), a training-free inference operator that treats phase shift as a nuisance variable. Given a patch stride, PPOM evaluates the identity view and reflection-padded translations, pairs opposite shifts into horizontal, vertical, and diagonal antithetic families, and assigns equal mass to these families and the identity prediction to avoid view-count bias during phase integration. In summary, PPOM provides a deterministic interface between prompt adaptation and patch-grid sensitivity. Across multiple prompt-learning hosts, PPOM improves host performance without re-training.

</details>


### [22] [Rethinking Auxiliary Modalities in Multimodal Zero-shot Anomaly Detection: From Semantic Fusion to Conditional Modulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13973)
*Peng Wu,Xin Ge,Yujia Sun,Guansong Pang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出一种即插即用的辅助模态条件增强框架，用于提升RGB基础模型的零样本异常检测能力，尤其针对几何、深度和细微表面异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RGB零样本异常检测依赖视觉-语言预训练，具备较强语义匹配能力，但对几何变形、深度变化和细微表面变化感知不足；直接多模态融合可能破坏RGB基础模型中已建立的文本对齐异常语义，并常需模态专用结构。

Method: 保留原有RGB图像-文本异常匹配路径，将辅助观测作为条件信号来精炼RGB特征。具体包括：轻量级元学习模块基于全局RGB与辅助表征生成样本自适应低秩残差更新；结合初始RGB异常响应与辅助模态可靠性构建不确定性感知空间调制，以决定局部残差增强或抑制的位置。

Result: 在MVTec 3D-AD和Eyecandies数据集上的实验表明，该框架能稳定提升多种主流RGB零样本异常检测器，并取得多模态零样本异常检测的先进性能。

Conclusion: 该方法通过全局到局部的条件调制，在不重建共享多模态语义空间的情况下实现选择性多模态增强，既利用辅助结构信息，又保持RGB模型原有异常语义，是一种通用且可扩展的ZSAD增强方案。

Abstract: Recent foundation model-based methods have endowed RGB images with strong zero-shot anomaly detection (ZSAD) through vision-language pretraining. However, RGB observations alone remain limited in perceiving anomalies dominated by geometric deformation, depth variation, or subtle surface changes. Auxiliary modalities can provide complementary structural information, but existing multimodal methods typically fuse them directly into a shared semantic space, which may disturb the text-aligned anomaly semantics established by RGB foundation models and often requires modality-specific architectures. To address this issue, we propose a plug-and-play auxiliary-conditioned enhancement framework for zero-shot anomaly detection. Instead of reconstructing a joint multimodal anomaly semantic space, our framework preserves the original RGB image-text anomaly matching pathway and uses auxiliary observations as conditional signals for RGB feature refinement, allowing auxiliary modalities to seamlessly enhance existing RGB-based zero-shot anomaly detectors. Specifically, a lightweight meta-learning module takes global RGB and auxiliary representations as input and generates sample-adaptive low-rank residual updates to determine how RGB features should be refined. We further construct uncertainty-aware spatial modulation from the initial RGB anomaly response and auxiliary reliability, which determines where local residual updates are strengthened or suppressed. This global-to-local conditional modulation enables selective multimodal enhancement while preserving the original RGB anomaly semantics. Extensive experiments on MVTec 3D-AD and Eyecandies demonstrate that our framework consistently improves multiple popular RGB-based zero-shot anomaly detectors, achieving state-of-the-art performance for multimodal zero-shot anomaly detection.

</details>


### [23] [ProFocus: Interpreting Affective Experience in Artistic Images with Progressive Visual Focusing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13974)
*Zhiyan Zhang,Zicheng Yan,Jianqi Chen,Peipei Song,Shanshan Wang,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProFocus 通过层次化语言先验与渐进式视觉聚焦机制，提升艺术图像情感识别与情感解释性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 艺术图像常通过抽象概念、视觉隐喻和细节安排激发观者情绪，情感线索比自然图像更细腻复杂；现有方法多依赖 CLIP 等通用视觉嵌入，难以充分捕捉艺术情感中的层次化语义与审美线索。

Method: 提出 ProFocus 框架，包含 Hierarchical Art Critic（HAC）和 Progressive Hint Fusion（PHF）。HAC 利用多模态大语言模型生成三个认知层级的结构化语言先验：氛围风格、叙事主体和具体细节；PHF 将这些层次化提示按顺序注入视觉特征，实现类似人类审美感知过程的渐进式聚焦。

Result: 在 ArtEmis v1.0 和 v2.0 数据集上进行实验，结果显示 ProFocus 在情感识别和情感解释生成方面均持续优于现有先进方法。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将人类审美认知层级建模为结构化语义提示，并与视觉特征进行渐进融合，有助于更准确地理解艺术图像中的细微情感线索并生成更可靠的解释。

Abstract: Interpreting the emotional responses triggered by images is central to achieving emotional intelligence. Compared with natural images, visual art is intentionally created to elicit emotional responses from its viewers through abstract concepts and visual metaphors, making affective interpretation particularly challenging. However, most existing methods rely on general-purpose visual embeddings (e.g., CLIP), failing to capture the nuanced cues underlying artistic emotion. To address this gap, we propose \textbf{ProFocus}, a novel framework that models affective experience in artistic images via progressive visual focusing. The key idea is to model visual representation learning inspired by a hierarchical cognitive theory of human aesthetic appreciation. Technically, ProFocus contains two core components: a Hierarchical Art Critic (HAC) and a Progressive Hint Fusion (PHF) module. HAC leverages multimodal large language models to generate structured linguistic priors at three cognitive levels--atmospheric style, narrative subjects, and concrete details--thereby translating artistic perception into coherent semantic guidance. Building upon these priors, PHF departs from conventional cross-modal fusion by sequentially injecting the hierarchical hints into visual features, enabling a progressive focusing process that mirrors human perception. This design allows the model to capture subtle affective cues and produce more faithful explanations. Extensive experiments on the ArtEmis v1.0 and v2.0 datasets demonstrate that ProFocus consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods in both emotion recognition and affective explanation. Project page: https://github.com/Zhang-Zhiyan/ProFocus.

</details>


### [24] [FIRM: Fine-Grained Intra-Token Representation of Masks for Remote Sensing Reasoning Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13980)
*Weidong Tang,Kaiyu Li,Yikai Wang,Yanan Wu,Haotian Gan,Shihong Wang,Xiangyong Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FIRM通过为每个视觉token预测细粒度的r×r子单元掩码编码，缓解遥感推理分割中小目标、细结构和邻近实例在token内部被合并导致的边界粗糙问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM将图像编码为合并多个patch的视觉token，并通常给每个token分配单一二值掩码标签；在遥感图像中，小目标、细长结构或相邻实例可能落在同一token内部，导致内部空间结构丢失、目标粘连和边界不精确。

Method: 提出FIRM（Fine-grained Intra-token Representation of Masks）：对每个视觉token预测一个表示r×r二值子单元模式的mask code，而非单一前景/背景标签；通过固定查找表将预测编码转为离散子单元掩码，并通过边缘化编码分布得到软结构场；进一步使用轻量级连续渲染器结合预合并视觉特征和图像细节细化子单元内部边界。

Result: 在五个卫星和无人机图像推理/指代表达分割基准上取得领先表现；例如在LaSeRS上达到70.5/80.5 gIoU/cIoU，在EarthReason上获得平均3.0个百分点提升。

Conclusion: 显式建模token内部的掩码空间模式能有效弥合MLLM视觉token表示与像素级分割需求之间的粒度差距，显著提升遥感场景下细粒度推理分割性能。

Abstract: Reasoning segmentation requires multimodal large language models (MLLMs) to translate implicit instructions into precise pixel-level masks. MLLMs encode an image as visual tokens, each of which merges a group of image patches. In remote sensing images, small targets, thin structures, and adjacent instances can occupy different parts of the same visual token. Assigning a single binary mask label to such a token loses its internal spatial structure, causing nearby targets to merge and object boundaries to become coarse. To bridge this representational gap, we introduce FIRM, a Fine-grained Intra-token Representation of Masks. For each visual token, FIRM predicts a mask code that specifies an $r\times r$ binary sub-cell pattern rather than a single foreground/background label. Given a target identified by the MLLM, the complete grid of mask codes is predicted in one mask pass. Fixed lookup converts the predicted codes into a discrete sub-cell mask, while marginalizing the code distribution yields a soft structural field. To further recover fine-grained boundaries within each sub-cell, we introduce a lightweight continuous renderer that refines this field using pre-merge visual features and image details. Across five reasoning and referring segmentation benchmarks on satellite and UAV images, FIRM achieves leading results, including $70.5/80.5$ gIoU/cIoU on LaSeRS and a $3.0$-point average gain on EarthReason. These results demonstrate the value of explicitly representing intra-token mask patterns for fine-grained MLLM segmentation.

</details>


### [25] [MedClaw: Heuristic Agent Harness for Long-Horizon Surgical Video Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14015)
*Yingying Fan,Penghui Du,Leyan Zhu,Runze He,Zimeng Wu,Yuxuan Zhang,Liang Chen,Jiahao Xie,Jiangtang Wang,Shuai Shao,Anchao Yang,Yutong Bai,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向长时程手术视频问答的智能体框架，通过文本编排器规划证据获取、冻结视觉语言子智能体执行视觉检索，并用无梯度的启发式技能蒸馏构建可复用技能库，在新基准 MedClawBench 上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长达数十分钟的手术视频需要跨阶段、跨时间的视觉证据推理。现有一次性 VLM 受上下文压缩限制易丢失关键细节；训练式视频智能体依赖大量数据且跨域泛化差，难以适应专业医学长视频。

Method: 作者将推理与感知解耦：文本编排器负责规划需要收集的证据并发出可审计工具调用；冻结的视觉语言子智能体负责查看、裁剪、检查帧和检索外部知识。进一步提出 reward-gated Heuristic Skill Distillation，从低分轨迹中挖掘候选技能，仅在提升验证奖励时保留，形成外部技能库，如 directed re-look。

Result: 仅用约 100 个标注样本即可适应任务；在包含 1,123 个问题的 MedClawBench 及保留的公开视频测试集上，该方法在两个数据集和四个评估维度上均稳定超过一次性 VLM 与通用视频智能体框架，尤其在长时、域外神经外科视频上提升最大。

Conclusion: 通过演化上下文与技能库而非微调模型权重，该框架为专业医学长视频理解提供了更数据高效、可审计、泛化性更强的方案。

Abstract: Understanding tens-of-minutes surgical videos requires long-horizon temporal reasoning, answering what happens before, after, or across stages of a procedure by grounding the question in visual evidence spread across time. Existing approaches handle this poorly: a one-shot vision-language model (VLM) compresses the whole procedure to fit its context window and loses the detail a "before" or "after" question depends on, while video agents that train the model where to look are data-hungry and transfer poorly to out-of-domain surgery. We build an agent harness that separates reasoning from perception and improves by evolving context rather than optimizing weights. A text-only orchestrator plans which evidence to gather and issues an auditable sequence of tool calls, while frozen vision-language sub-agents execute each call over the pixels, viewing, cropping, inspecting frames, and retrieving external knowledge. We further propose a gradient-free, reward-gated Heuristic Skill Distillation loop that mines the agent's own low-scoring traces and keeps a candidate skill only when it raises a validation reward, yielding reusable retrieval skills, notably directed re-look. Growing an external skill library rather than tuning weights, the loop adapts from only about 100 labeled examples, far fewer than supervised or reinforcement fine-tuning requires. To evaluate this agent, we introduce MedClawBench, a de-leaked, doctor-grounded benchmark of 1,123 questions over self-built long neurosurgery recordings and a held-out public lecture-video test split. Across both datasets and all four evaluation dimensions, our agent consistently outperforms one-shot VLMs and general video-agent frameworks, with the largest gains on the long, out-of-domain neurosurgery videos. Project page: https://fyycs.github.io/medclaw/.

</details>


### [26] [Content Based Video Narration of Gameplay with Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14016)
*Mathew Varghese*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需游戏特定遥测、引擎接入或专项训练的游戏视频自动解说系统，使用通用视觉语言模型与语音合成生成类似电竞直播的口播解说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通游戏录像缺乏实时解说，而现有高质量解说主要存在于专业电竞转播；作者希望为任意游戏录像提供可复现、低接入成本的内容驱动解说方案。

Method: 系统包含三项核心机制：将九帧均匀采样画面拼成3x3时间马赛克以降低VLM输入负载并保留运动线索；用最近K段解说作为上下文提示以减少静态场景重复描述；通过时长条件生成与弹性音频对齐，使合成语音精确填充视频片段时间槽。语音端支持云端TTS或Apple silicon上的6-bit量化4B本地TTS模型。

Result: 作者在即时战略游戏录像上进行了定性案例研究，并给出成本模型，显示时间马赛克可将每分钟图像负载降低9倍；同时记录了若干失败模式，包括游戏状态幻觉、马赛克导致的分辨率损失和音频变速造成的韵律伪影。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可复现的游戏视频自动解说基线系统，展示了通用VLM结合TTS在无专门数据和无游戏内信息条件下生成电竞风格解说的可行性，并提出后续定量评估协议。

Abstract: Live game commentary is scarce: it exists for professional esports broadcasts and almost nowhere else. We present a content-based video narration system that produces spoken, esports-style commentary for arbitrary gameplay recordings using a general-purpose vision-language model (VLM) and a text-to-speech back end, with no game-specific instrumentation, no engine telemetry, and no task-specific training. Three mechanisms carry the system. Temporal mosaic packing arranges nine uniformly sampled frames into a single 3x3 image, letting an image-native VLM reason about motion while consuming one image payload per segment instead of nine. Context-conditioned prompting replays the K most recent narrations as assistant-role history, suppressing the repetition that dominates per-segment captioning of static scenes. Duration-conditioned generation and elastic alignment constrain narration length in the prompt, then time-scale or symmetrically pad the synthesized audio so each utterance fills its segment slot exactly, giving frame-accurate muxing without a forced aligner. The implementation supports either cloud TTS or a 6-bit quantized 4B-parameter on-device TTS model on Apple silicon, making the speech stage fully local. We report a qualitative case study on real-time strategy footage, a cost model showing the mosaic reduces per-minute image payloads by 9x, and a candid account of observed failure modes - hallucinated game state, resolution loss from mosaicking, and prosody artifacts from time-scaling. We release the system as a reproducible baseline, with an evaluation protocol for the quantitative study a full version will report.

</details>


### [27] [ForgeWM: Progressive Causal Training for Few-Step Action-Conditioned Video World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14022)
*Xinye Li,Lingshuai Lin,Lei Wang,Liuzhou Zhang,Jialin Cui,Qingshan Li,Guanchu Wang,Qingbin Liu,Xi Chen,Jiang Bian,Wai Lam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForgeWM 将双向动作条件视频生成器逐步转化为低延迟、少步数的交互式视频世界模型，在保持控制响应可靠性的同时显著降低推理步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件视频世界模型需要满足交互场景中的低延迟因果生成，并准确响应键盘、鼠标或手柄等原生控制信号；但在因果蒸馏和自回归展开中，离散动作与连续鼠标运动很难与时间压缩潜变量块保持对齐。

Method: 提出 ForgeWM 渐进式框架，包括领域适配、教师强制因果训练、因果一致性蒸馏，以及使用双向教师模型进行在策略分布匹配；训练得到 1、2、4 步去噪预算的学生模型，并支持交互时低延迟生成与回放时重噪细化的双路径部署。

Result: 在配对 Minecraft 轨迹上，ForgeWM 在成像质量、参考对齐的运动轨迹一致性、动作符号准确率、鼠标控制准确率等指标上领先，并取得最低参考 LPIPS；同样流程可迁移到手柄控制的 FPS 场景。回放时细化可达到四步参考质量，同时相较从噪声重新生成更接近实际体验轨迹约三倍。

Conclusion: ForgeWM 证明了通过渐进式因果训练与蒸馏，可以构建可控、低延迟、少步数的交互式视频世界模型，并兼顾实时交互质量与回放质量提升。

Abstract: Action-conditioned video world models require low-latency causal generation and reliable responses to game-native controls. Although causal distillation enables one- or few-step video synthesis, extending it to interactive world models remains challenging, as discrete keyboard states and continuous mouse motion must remain aligned with temporally compressed latent chunks during causal training and autoregressive rollout. We introduce ForgeWM, a progressive framework that transforms a bidirectional action-conditioned video generator into efficient few-step world models through domain adaptation, teacher-forced causal training, causal consistency distillation, and on-policy distribution matching with a bidirectional teacher. The resulting budget-specialized students operate at steady-state denoising budgets of 1, 2, and 4 steps. ForgeWM further supports a dual-path deployment protocol combining latency-critical interaction with optional replay-time refinement, where the one-step student re-noises and refines its saved draft. On paired Minecraft trajectories, ForgeWM leads the evaluated systems in Imaging Quality, reference-aligned motion-profile agreement, action-sign accuracy, and mouse-control accuracy, while achieving the lowest reference LPIPS; the same four-stage recipe transfers to gamepad-controlled FPS gameplay. Replay-time refinement matches four-step reference quality while remaining roughly three times closer to the experienced trajectory than regeneration from noise. These results demonstrate ForgeWM's effectiveness for controllable few-step video generation.

</details>


### [28] [SSP: An Event-Matched Syn2Sim2Phy Cross-Domain Evaluation Framework for Autonomous Driving VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14024)
*Haojie Feng,Peizhi Zhang,Xinrui Zhang,Zhuoren Li,Junpeng Huang,Xiurong Wang,Dongxiao Yin,Yuxiang Zhang,Junfan Zhu,Lu Xiong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出SSP事件匹配的Syn2Sim2Phy评估框架，用于在合成、仿真与物理域中公平评估自动驾驶VLA模型，避免场景内容差异混淆域敏感性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA自动驾驶评估常分别选取合成、仿真和物理数据，性能差异可能来自场景内容变化，而非模型对不同域的真实敏感性，因此需要一种跨域事件一致的评估框架。

Method: 从合成长尾安全关键视频出发，构建经验证的事件规范，保持道路拓扑、参与者角色、相对运动、冲突演化、通行顺序、响应约束和事件阶段；再在CARLA与封闭试验场中实现对应场景，并通过迁移审计确认关键事件属性保留。对OpenEMMA、LLaViDA和Alpamayo-R1的异构输出映射到统一语义槽与1秒轨迹窗口，评估输出有效性、语义准确性、关键交互识别、轨迹质量和风险响应。

Result: 在Cut-in和弱势道路使用者穿越案例中，宏平均Integrated VLA Capability Scores分别为合成域0.259、仿真域0.291、物理域0.325，但最佳域随场景变化。模型层面，Alpamayo-R1得分0.405，OpenEMMA为0.338，LLaViDA为0.131。

Conclusion: SSP提供了可复现的场景迁移链和基于证据的VLA行为评估方式，能够更公平地分析跨域表现，并表明不能预设物理域一定优于其他域。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models for autonomous driving jointly produce scene interpretation, language-based reasoning, and driving trajectories. Existing evaluations often use independently selected synthetic, simulated, and physical data, so measured performance gaps can be confounded by changes in scenario content rather than genuine domain sensitivity. We propose SSP (Synthetic-Simulation-Physical), an event-matched Syn2Sim2Phy evaluation framework that anchors cross-domain comparison to the same safety-critical interaction. Starting from a synthetic long-tail video, SSP builds a validated event specification that preserves road topology, participant roles, relative motion, conflict evolution, passing order, response constraints, and event phases. Platform-specific realizations are then constructed in CARLA and on a closed proving ground and are evaluated only after transfer audits confirm preservation of mandatory event properties. SSP maps heterogeneous outputs from OpenEMMA, LLaViDA, and Alpamayo-R1 into common semantic slots and a 1 s trajectory window to assess output validity, semantic accuracy, critical-interaction recognition, trajectory quality, and risk response. Across Cut-in and vulnerable-road-user crossing cases, the macro-averaged Integrated VLA Capability Scores are 0.259, 0.291, and 0.325 in the Synthetic, Simulation, and Physical domains, respectively, while the best domain varies by scenario. Alpamayo-R1, OpenEMMA, and LLaViDA obtain scores of 0.405, 0.338, and 0.131. SSP provides a reproducible scene-transfer chain and an evidence-qualified evaluation of VLA behavior without assuming that the Physical domain is universally superior.

</details>


### [29] [E-S2Feat:Semantic-Guided Spiking Local Feature Detection and Description for Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14027)
*Yang Yi,Juntao Hua,Jinpu Zhang,Liangwei Fan,Hui Shen,Dewen Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 E-S2Feat，一种面向事件相机局部特征检测与描述的脉冲神经网络框架，在保持精度的同时显著提升理论计算能效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有高时间分辨率和高动态范围，但事件稀疏、噪声以及纹理信息有限会影响局部特征学习的鲁棒性；同时，在无人机等资源受限平台上部署时，需要兼顾精度与能耗。

Method: 方法从特征表示与特征选择两个角度联合优化：提出模块特定的脉冲激活机制，在低比特、能效友好的推理条件下保留细粒度结构线索和判别信息；进一步引入语义引导的特征调制机制，利用语义先验优化关键点响应分布并增强局部描述子的判别性。

Result: 在 ECD 和 EDS 数据集上，E-S2Feat 在位姿估计精度方面显著优于 SuperEvent 等基线方法；与对应的人工神经网络版本相比精度相当，同时理论计算能效约提升 4.8 倍；在 TUM-VIE 上的视觉惯性里程计实验进一步验证了其在完整 SLAM 系统中的有效性。

Conclusion: E-S2Feat 证明了脉冲神经网络可用于高效且鲁棒的事件相机局部特征学习，适合资源受限场景中的低功耗视觉定位与 SLAM 应用。

Abstract: Benefiting from high temporal resolution and dynamic range, event-based local feature methods have attracted increasing attention. However, event sparsity, noise, and limited texture still hinder robust local feature learning. Deploying such methods on resource-constrained platforms such as unmanned aerial vehicles also requires balancing accuracy and energy efficiency. To address these challenges, this paper proposes \textbf{E-S2Feat}, a spiking neural network framework for event-based local feature detection and description. The framework jointly optimizes local feature learning from the perspectives of feature representation and selection. First, a module-specific spiking activation mechanism preserves fine-grained structural cues and discriminative information under low-bit, energy-efficient inference, thereby improving overall representation fidelity. Furthermore, a semantic-guided feature modulation mechanism leverages semantic priors to refine keypoint response distributions and enhance local descriptor discriminability, thereby guiding the model to extract local features with greater geometric stability and stronger discriminative capability. Experiments on the ECD and EDS datasets show that the proposed method significantly outperforms baseline methods such as SuperEvent in pose estimation accuracy. It also achieves accuracy comparable to its artificial neural network counterpart while delivering an approximately 4.8-fold improvement in theoretical computational energy efficiency. Visual-inertial odometry experiments on the TUM-VIE dataset further verify the effectiveness and practical application potential of the proposed method in complete SLAM systems.

</details>


### [30] [Beyond Text Conditioning: A Systematic Study of MLLM-DiT Fusion for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14043)
*Yanbo Ding,Yijia Fan,Caihua Shan,Yifan Yang,Yifei Shen,Weijie Wang,Xirui Hu,Dongsheng Li,Lili Qiu,Yuqing Yang,Yali Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 BiVidGen：让 MLLM 先生成离散语义视觉 token 进行高层视频规划，再由 DiT 通过多层交叉注意力条件渲染视频，从而提升文本到视频生成的语义对齐与时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 DiT 在高保真视频生成中表现突出，但高层语义规划能力有限；而 MLLM 与扩散模型的混合架构在图像生成中有效，在视频生成中仍探索不足，且常把 MLLM 仅作为冻结特征编码器，未充分利用其语义生成能力。

Method: 论文系统研究 MLLM 与 DiT 的集成方式，围绕中间表示、生成方式和注入方式展开。最终采用 EMA tokenizer 产生的离散语义视觉 token 作为桥接表示，由 MLLM 以自回归因果建模生成这些 token，再让 DiT 在扩散渲染阶段通过多层交叉注意力同时条件于文本和视觉 token。

Result: 实验表明，相比微调 DiT 基线，BiVidGen 在语义对齐和时序一致性方面更优，并在 VBench-Long 上取得更强表现。

Conclusion: 显式的 MLLM 视觉规划可作为文本到视频生成中优于纯文本条件的有效中间接口，有助于弥补 DiT 在高层语义规划方面的不足。

Abstract: Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) have become the dominant paradigm for high-fidelity video generation, yet their ability to perform high-level semantic planning remains limited. While hybrid architectures integrating MLLMs with diffusion backbones have shown strong advantages in image synthesis, such designs remain underexplored in video generation, where existing approaches often treat MLLMs primarily as frozen feature encoders rather than semantic generators. To fill this gap, we systematically study how an MLLM should be integrated with a DiT for video generation by answering three questions: what intermediate representation should bridge the MLLM and DiT, how the MLLM should generate it, and how the DiT should incorporate it during diffusion rendering. Our analysis reveals three key findings: (1) discrete semantic visual tokens produced by an EMA-based tokenizer provide a stable and expressive interface, (2) autoregressive causal modeling is effective for generating these tokens, and (3) explicit visual-token conditioning is more effective than prompt refinement or latent bridging. Based on these findings, we propose BiVidGen, a hybrid framework where an MLLM first generates semantic visual tokens and a DiT renders videos conditioned on both text and these tokens via multi-layer cross-attention. Extensive experiments show that BiVidGen improves semantic alignment and temporal coherence over a fine-tuned DiT baseline, achieving stronger performance on VBench-Long. These results demonstrate that explicit MLLM-based visual planning provides an effective intermediate interface for text-to-video generation beyond text-only conditioning.

</details>


### [31] [Source-Agnostic Image Translation Based on Latent Aware Adaptive Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14046)
*Tomislav Dobrički,Byung-Woo Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需源域训练的扩散模型图像到图像翻译框架，通过在反向扩散过程中动态细化二值掩码，实现更精准的区域定位与高保真翻译。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督图像到图像方法常依赖固定阈值或额外训练，难以适应扩散过程中不同噪声水平下模型预测置信度的变化，导致域特定区域定位不准或全局结构被破坏。

Method: 利用目标域预训练扩散模型，在每个潜变量时间步计算模型预测差异，并基于目标分布噪声潜图像上的差异均值与标准差构造时间相关的统计阈值，从而动态更新二值掩码，隔离域特定区域并保持结构一致性。

Result: 在 AFHQ 和 Celeba-HQ 数据集上，该方法在真实性指标 FID、KID 以及忠实度指标 SSIM、LPIPS 上优于现有先进的无监督 Image-to-Image 方法。

Conclusion: 该框架仅需目标域预训练模型，无需专门训练或源域假设，即可实现跨多种源分布的自动区域定位与自然图像翻译，具有较强的通用性和实用价值。

Abstract: In this work, we propose a source-agnostic framework that dynamically refines a binary mask throughout the reverse diffusion process by computing the discrepancies of a pretrained diffusion model's prediction for each latent time step. Rather than relying on a fixed threshold, our method introduces a time-dependent statistical thresholding scheme derived from the empirical mean and standard deviation of prediction discrepancies across the latent noisy images from the target distribution. This allows the mask to adapt to the model's varying predictive confidence at different noise levels, effectively isolating domain-specific regions while preserving global structural coherence. Experimental results on the AFHQ and Celeba-HQ datasets demonstrate that our approach outperforms state-of-the-art unsupervised Image-to-Image methods in both realism (FID, KID) and faithfulness (SSIM, LPIPS). By requiring only a pretrained model of the target domain, our approach enables precise, automated localization and seamless translation across diverse source distributions without any specialized training. The project source code is available at: https://github.com/dtoma95/PM-Edit

</details>


### [32] [Discovery and Spatial Characterisation of Multiple Shortcut Groups for Auditing Vision Model Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14051)
*Akshit Achara,Vishnunarayan Manickam,Thomas Day,Esther Puyol Anton,Alexander Hammers,Andrew P. King*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出将单张图像的捷径贡献图与任务贡献图进行聚类分组，以发现数据集中反复出现但可能只存在于局部子集的空间捷径模式，并据此进行错误子集检查与干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有方法通过归因图分析伪相关/捷径学习的空间位置，但通常在整个数据集上聚合贡献图，容易掩盖只在部分图像中重复出现的捷径模式。因此需要一种能定位并分组这些子集级空间模式的方法。

Method: 基于条件对齐类方法获得每张图像的 shortcut-aligned 与 task-aligned contribution maps；随后使用 K-means 与非负矩阵分解（NMF）对这些贡献图进行分组，形成 recurring spatial patterns；通过贡献图和代表样本可视化不同捷径组，并进一步进行输入遮挡与测试时内部特征干预。

Result: 在 CelebA、CheXpert、Waterbirds、Camelyon17、ISIC2019 等数据集以及 ResNet、ViT 模型上，方法发现了共享和差异化的捷径/任务空间贡献模式；不同组具有不同的子群构成与错误率，可用于定位高错误率图像子集。遮挡或抑制任务贡献区域会显著降低分类性能。

Conclusion: 对子集级贡献图进行聚类能更细粒度地揭示捷径学习的空间结构，并支持针对高风险子集的检查；结合捷径抑制与任务放大的特征干预通常可降低不同子群之间的性能差异。

Abstract: Deep learning models trained on datasets with spurious correlations can achieve high average accuracy whilst relying on shortcut features that do not generalise out of distribution. Whilst out-of-distribution testing highlights subgroup performance disparities arising from shortcut learning, it does not localise the regions within images that are associated with it. Existing research mostly uses attribution maps from interpretability methods to understand the spatial nature of spurious correlations. For example, conditional alignment methods separate task-relevant evidence from evidence tied to spurious correlations by comparing attribution maps from a task model, a sensitive attribute model, and a bias-reduced reference model. This yields shortcut-aligned and task-aligned contribution maps for each image. However, existing methods aggregate these maps across the dataset, potentially masking recurring spatial shortcut patterns that occur only in subsets of images. We address this limitation by grouping per-image shortcut and task contribution maps into recurring spatial patterns using K-means and non-negative matrix factorisation, and visualising the resulting shortcut groups through contribution maps and representative examples. Across CelebA, CheXpert, Waterbirds, Camelyon17, and ISIC2019, and across ResNet and ViT models, the discovered shortcut groups reveal both shared and distinct spatial patterns of shortcut and task contribution, with varying subgroup composition and error rates, enabling targeted inspection of image subsets with higher error rates. We perform input occlusion and internal test-time interventions to show that masking or suppressing task contribution regions substantially degrades the model classification performance and propose a combined shortcut suppression and task amplification feature intervention approach which generally reduces performance disparities.

</details>


### [33] [Voxel-based 3D Facies Segmentation from Seismic Data: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14058)
*Duc-Thanh Pham,Minh-Tan Pham,Anh Nguyen,Van Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出并基准化体素级3D地震相分割任务，使用公开3D地震数据集和标准评测流程，对现代3D分割架构进行比较，强调3D连续性建模的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地震相分割研究多将3D地震体预处理为2D切片并作为2D分割问题处理，容易造成切片间不连续，无法充分保留3D空间结构与地质连续性；同时该领域缺少可复现、标准化的3D体素级基准。

Method: 构建一个可复现的体素级3D地震相分割benchmark，基于Netherlands F3和Parihaka等公开地震体数据，设计标准化的数据划分与评价指标，并评估三类代表性的现代3D分割架构以建立基线。

Result: 实验建立了较强的3D地震相分割基线结果，表明3D分割模型在学习连续地质模式方面具有潜力，同时揭示了在有限标注数据条件下仍存在性能与泛化挑战。

Conclusion: 该工作推动地震相分割从2D切片方法转向更符合数据本质的3D体素级建模，并通过公开、标准化的benchmark为后续方法比较和研究发展提供基础。

Abstract: Seismic facies segmentation has emerged as a significant challenge in geophysics, requiring robust methods and systems to effectively identify geologically analogous facies with limited labeled data. Although existing studies have shown promising results in 2D facies segmentation, they often preprocess the original 3D seismic volumes into sets of 2D slices, typically the inline and crossline directions, and treat this problem as a purely 2D segmentation task. This simplification introduces discontinuities across slices and fails to preserve the spatial and structural continuity in 3D seismic data, thus limiting the model's ability to learn coherent geological patterns. In this work, we present a comparative and reproducible benchmark for voxel-based 3D seismic facies segmentation, built upon publicly available seismic volumes including the Netherlands F3 and the Parihaka datasets, with standardized data splits and evaluation metrics. By evaluating the three representative families of modern 3D segmentation architectures, we establish strong baseline results that highlight the potential and remaining challenges for future research in this domain.

</details>


### [34] [InstructVVT: Instruction-Driven Video Virtual Try-On without Auxiliary Spatial Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14070)
*Dingbao Shao,Song Wu,Xinyu Chen,Qian Wang,Jiahang Li,Kuai Jiang,Jiang Lin,Yuhang Liu,Ziyu Chen,Duo Li,Jiaxin Hu,Shengrong Gu,Ziheng Tang,Rongrong Liu,Yanlun Peng,Liang Li,Junlan Feng,Lujia Jin,Ting Zhang,Jian Yang,Zili Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InstructVVT 是一种无需推理阶段空间先验的视频虚拟试衣框架，通过指令驱动与参考服装引导，在服装保真、结构保持和时间一致性方面优于现有开源方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频虚拟试衣需要精确替换人物服装，同时保持原视频空间结构与时间动态。现有方法依赖掩码、姿态等手工空间先验，但这些先验在真实无约束视频中易失效，并会丢失丰富视觉上下文；同时，常规重建损失难以刻画试衣任务中的人类偏好。

Method: 提出基于 Diffusion Transformer 的 InstructVVT 框架，以源视频、参考服装和文本指令为输入，不依赖推理阶段空间先验。方法包含双层参考条件机制：使用多模态大语言模型推断语义编辑 token，用于目标消歧与结构保持；使用轻量级条件通路显式注入细粒度服装视觉细节。进一步设计试衣特定奖励，并采用 DiffusionNFT 算法进行人类偏好对齐。

Result: 在 ViViD-S 和 TripVVT-Bench 上的大量实验表明，该方法在服装保真度、结构保持和时间一致性方面超过最先进的开源方法，同时需要更少的推理时控制信号。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，视频虚拟试衣中的细粒度编辑控制可以从输入三元组中自动恢复，而不必依赖易失效的手工空间先验；结合参考条件与偏好对齐可显著提升真实场景下的视频试衣质量。

Abstract: Video virtual try-on is a highly constrained editing task requiring the precise replacement of a target person's clothing while strictly preserving the original video's spatial structure and temporal dynamics. Existing methods heavily rely on auxiliary handcrafted spatial priors (e.g., masks, poses) for editing control. However, these priors are prone to failure in unconstrained real-world videos and often compress rich visual context into incomplete structural signals. Furthermore, standard reconstruction objectives fail to fully capture try-on-specific human preferences. To address these challenges, we propose InstructVVT, an instruction-driven and reference-guided video virtual try-on framework based on a Diffusion Transformer (DiT) that operates without inference-time spatial priors. Our core insight is to recover fine-grained control directly from the input triplet (source video, reference garment, and instruction) via a dual-level reference conditioning scheme. Specifically, an MLLM infers semantic edit tokens for target disambiguation and structural preservation, while a lightweight conditioning pathway explicitly injects fine-grained visual garment details. Finally, we design a try-on-specific reward and utilize the DiffusionNFT algorithm to align the model with human preferences. Extensive experiments on ViViD-S and TripVVT-Bench demonstrate that InstructVVT outperforms state-of-the-art open-source methods in garment fidelity, structural preservation, and temporal consistency, despite requiring fewer inference-time controls.

</details>


### [35] [Owner3D: Ownership-Guided Style Writing for Training-Free Localized 3D Stylization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14078)
*Suchang Tao,Kaifeng Shi,Zhiyan Liu,Zhuoyuan Jiang,Yuqi Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Owner3D 是一种免训练的局部 3D 风格化框架，可在大重建模型中只改变目标部件外观，同时显著抑制风格泄漏并保留非目标区域外观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LRM 中风格通常在渲染前注入中间外观表示，而紧凑的 triplane 特征会被目标与非目标表面共享，导致局部风格化时出现风格泄漏和语义边界模糊。

Method: 提出 ownership-guided style writing，将参考风格注入限制在目标区域；使用 boundary dual slots 在边界附近分别维护目标与非目标局部特征源；采用 surface-first texture readout，分层融合表面、3D 与 triplane 的 ownership 证据，以在可见性不完整时稳健读取纹理。

Result: 在基于 Google Scanned Objects 和 PartNet 构建的基准上，Owner3D 在目标区域风格保真度和非目标外观保持方面优于现有方法；相比 StyleSplat 和 LAENeRF，外观泄漏分别降低 86.4% 和 89.9%。

Conclusion: Owner3D 通过将局部外观控制直接嵌入 LRM 重建过程，在无需额外训练的情况下实现更精确、边界更清晰且泄漏更少的局部 3D 风格化。
